{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ ViewCompat.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener(findViewById(R.id.main),\ (v,\ insets)\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Insets\ systemBars\ =\ insets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars());} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ v.setPadding(systemBars.left,\ systemBars.top,\ systemBars.right,\ systemBars.bottom);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ return\ insets;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \});} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ bookid\ =\ findViewById(R.id.bookid);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ bookname\ =\ findViewById(R.id.bookname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ authorname\ =\ findViewById(R.id.authorname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ pubname\ =\ findViewById(R.id.pubname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ publishyear\ =\ findViewById(R.id.publishyear);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ price\ =\ findViewById(R.id.price);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ stock\ =\ findViewById(R.id.stock);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ insert\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnInsert);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ view\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnView);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ DB\ =\ new\ DBHelper(this);} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ insert.setOnClickListener(view\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ idTXT\ =\ bookid.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ nameTXT\ =\ bookname.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ authorTXT\ =\ authorname.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ pubTXT\ =\ pubname.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ yearTXT\ =\ publishyear.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ priceTXT\ =\ price.getText().toString();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ String\ stockTXT\ =\ stock.getText().toString();} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ if\ (idTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ nameTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ authorTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ pubTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ yearTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ priceTXT.isEmpty()\ ||\ stockTXT.isEmpty())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Please\ enter\ all\ the\ details",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Boolean\ checkInsertData\ =\ DB.insertBookData(idTXT,\ nameTXT,\ authorTXT,\ pubTXT,\ yearTXT,\ priceTXT,\ stockTXT);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ if\ (checkInsertData)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "New\ Entry\ Inserted",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ //\ Clear\ fields\ after\ insertion} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ bookid.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ bookname.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ authorname.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ pubname.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ publishyear.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ price.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ stock.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "New\ Entry\ Not\ Inserted",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \});} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ view.setOnClickListener(view\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Cursor\ res\ =\ DB.getdata();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ if\ (res.getCount()\ ==\ 0)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "No\ Entry\ Exists",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ StringBuilder\ buffer\ =\ new\ StringBuilder();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ while\ (res.moveToNext())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Book\ ID:\ ").append(res.getString(0)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Book\ Name:\ ").append(res.getString(1)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Author\ Name:\ ").append(res.getString(2)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Publisher:\ ").append(res.getString(3)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Year:\ ").append(res.getString(4)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Price:\ ").append(res.getString(5)).append("\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ buffer.append("Stock:\ ").append(res.getString(6)).append("\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ AlertDialog.Builder\ builder\ =\ new\ AlertDialog.Builder(MainActivity.this);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ builder.setCancelable(true);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ builder.setTitle("Book\ Details");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ builder.setMessage(buffer.toString());} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ builder.show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.librarydatabase;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.content.ContentValues;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.content.Context;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.Cursor;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ DBHelper\ extends\ SQLiteOpenHelper\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ public\ DBHelper(Context\ context)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ super(context,\ "Library.db",\ null,\ 1);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ public\ void\ onCreate(SQLiteDatabase\ DB)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ DB.execSQL("create\ Table\ BookDetails(bookid\ TEXT\ primary\ key,\ bookname\ TEXT,\ authorname\ TEXT,\ pubname\ TEXT,\ publishyear\ TEXT,\ price\ TEXT,\ stock\ TEXT)");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ public\ void\ onUpgrade(SQLiteDatabase\ DB,\ int\ i,\ int\ i1)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ DB.execSQL("drop\ Table\ if\ exists\ BookDetails");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ public\ Boolean\ insertBookData(String\ bookid,\ String\ bookname,\ String\ authorname,\ String\ pubname,\ String\ publishyear,\ String\ price,\ String\ stock)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ SQLiteDatabase\ DB\ =\ this.getWritableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ ContentValues\ contentValues\ =\ new\ ContentValues();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("bookid",\ bookid);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("bookname",\ bookname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("authorname",\ authorname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("pubname",\ pubname);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("publishyear",\ publishyear);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("price",\ price);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ contentValues.put("stock",\ stock);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ long\ result\ =\ DB.insert("BookDetails",\ null,\ contentValues);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ return\ result\ !=\ -1;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ public\ Cursor\ getdata()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ SQLiteDatabase\ DB\ =\ this.getWritableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ return\ DB.rawQuery("Select\ *\ from\ BookDetails",\ null);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \ \ \ \ \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}Program Title : Create an app to display product details using GridView and ListView from SQLite database. \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.pg27\_dbview;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.Cursor;\ import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.ArrayAdapter;\ import\ android.widget.Button;\ import\ android.widget.EditText;\ import\ android.widget.GridView;\ import\ android.widget.ListView;\ import\ android.widget.Toast;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ java.util.ArrayList;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ \{\ DBHelper\ dbHelper;} \\
{\ttfamily\small EditText\ name;\ Button\ submit;\ ListView\ list;\ GridView\ grid;} \\
{\ttfamily\small ArrayList\textless{}String\textgreater{}\ productList;\ ArrayAdapter\textless{}String\textgreater{}\ adapter;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{\ super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);\ setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small dbHelper\ =\ new\ DBHelper(this);\ name\ =\ findViewById(R.id.name);\ submit\ =\ findViewById(R.id.submit);\ list\ =\ findViewById(R.id.list);} \\
{\ttfamily\small grid\ =\ findViewById(R.id.grid);} \\
{\ttfamily\small productList\ =\ new\ ArrayList\textless{}\textgreater{}();} \\
{\ttfamily\small adapter\ =\ new\ ArrayAdapter\textless{}\textgreater{}(this,\ android.R.layout.simple\_list\_item\_1,\ productList);\ list.setAdapter(adapter);} \\
{\ttfamily\small grid.setAdapter(adapter);\ loadProducts();} \\
{\ttfamily\small submit.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ pName\ =\ name.getText().toString().trim();\ if\ (!pName.isEmpty())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small dbHelper.insertProduct(pName);} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Product\ added",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();\ name.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small loadProducts();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Enter\ name",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ loadProducts()\ \{\ productList.clear();} \\
{\ttfamily\small Cursor\ cursor\ =\ dbHelper.getAllProducts();\ while\ (cursor.moveToNext())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small productList.add(cursor.getString(1));} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small cursor.close();\ adapter.notifyDataSetChanged();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{SecondaryActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small productList\ =\ new\ ArrayList\textless{}\textgreater{}();} \\
{\ttfamily\small adapter\ =\ new\ ArrayAdapter\textless{}\textgreater{}(this,\ android.R.layout.simple\_list\_item\_1,\ productList);\ list.setAdapter(adapter);} \\
{\ttfamily\small grid.setAdapter(adapter);\ loadProducts();} \\
{\ttfamily\small submit.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ pName\ =\ name.getText().toString().trim();\ if\ (!pName.isEmpty())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small dbHelper.insertProduct(pName);} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Product\ added",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();\ name.setText("");} \\
{\ttfamily\small loadProducts();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Enter\ name",\ Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ loadProducts()\ \{\ productList.clear();} \\
{\ttfamily\small Cursor\ cursor\ =\ dbHelper.getAllProducts();\ while\ (cursor.moveToNext())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small productList.add(cursor.getString(1));} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small cursor.close();\ adapter.notifyDataSetChanged();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ DBHelper\ extends\ SQLiteOpenHelper\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ static\ final\ String\ DATABASE\_NAME\ =\ "ProductDB.db";\ private\ static\ final\ int\ DATABASE\_VERSION\ =\ 1;} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ static\ final\ String\ TABLE\_PRODUCTS\ =\ "products";\ public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_ID\ =\ "id";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_NAME\ =\ "name";} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ DBHelper(Context\ context)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super(context,\ DATABASE\_NAME,\ null,\ DATABASE\_VERSION);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onCreate(SQLiteDatabase\ db)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ createTable\ =\ "CREATE\ TABLE\ "\ +\ TABLE\_PRODUCTS\ +\ "\ ("\ +\ COL\_ID\ +\ "\ INTEGER\ PRIMARY\ KEY\ AUTOINCREMENT,\ "\ +\ COL\_NAME\ +\ "\ TEXT)";} \\
{\ttfamily\small db.execSQL(createTable);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onUpgrade(SQLiteDatabase\ db,\ int\ oldVersion,\ int\ newVersion)\ \{\ db.execSQL("DROP\ TABLE\ IF\ EXISTS\ "\ +\ TABLE\_PRODUCTS);} \\
{\ttfamily\small onCreate(db);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ long\ insertProduct(String\ name)\ \{\ SQLiteDatabase\ db\ =\ this.getWritableDatabase();\ ContentValues\ values\ =\ new\ ContentValues();\ values.put(COL\_NAME,\ name);} \\
{\ttfamily\small long\ id\ =\ db.insert(TABLE\_PRODUCTS,\ null,\ values);\ db.close();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return\ id;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ Cursor\ getAllProducts()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small SQLiteDatabase\ db\ =\ this.getReadableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return\ db.rawQuery("SELECT\ *\ FROM\ "\ +\ TABLE\_PRODUCTS,\ null);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

\hline
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}?xml\ version="1.0"\ encoding="utf-8"?\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}androidx.constraintlayout.widget.ConstraintLayout\ xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"} \\
{\ttfamily\small xmlns:app="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res-auto"\ xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools"\ android:id="@+id/main"\ android:layout\_width="match\_parent"\ android:layout\_height="match\_parent"\ android:padding="16dp"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:paddingTop="48dp"\ tools:context=".MainActivity"\textgreater{}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}EditText\ android:id="@+id/name"\ android:layout\_width="0dp"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"\ android:layout\_marginTop="24dp"\ android:layout\_marginEnd="8dp"\ android:hint="Enter\ product\ name"\ android:inputType="text"\ app:layout\_constraintEnd\_toStartOf="@+id/submit"\ app:layout\_constraintStart\_toStartOf="parent"\ app:layout\_constraintTop\_toTopOf="parent"\ /\textgreater{}} \\

{\ttfamily\small btnIncrement.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small count++;} \\

{\ttfamily\small \});} \\

{\ttfamily\small tvCount.setText(String.valueOf(count));} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\

{\ttfamily\small count\ =\ 0;} \\

{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}?xml\ version="1.0"\ encoding="utf-8"?\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}LinearLayout\ xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="match\_parent"} \\

\strut \\

{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"} \\

{\ttfamily\small android:text="Speak"/\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}Button} \\

{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"} \\

{\ttfamily\small android:text="Stop"/\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}/LinearLayout\textgreater{}} \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{activity\_main.xml:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.texttospeech;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.text.TextUtils;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Button;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.EditText;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Toast;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ java.util.Locale;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ implements} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small TextToSpeech.OnInitListener\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small EditText\ etText;} \\
{\ttfamily\small Button\ btnSpeak,\ btnStop;} \\
{\ttfamily\small TextToSpeech\ textToSpeech;} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ isTtsReady\ =\ false;} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
{\ttfamily\small etText\ =\ findViewById(R.id.etText);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnSpeak\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnSpeak);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStop\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnStop);} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Initialize\ TextToSpeech\ engine} \\
{\ttfamily\small textToSpeech\ =\ new\ TextToSpeech(this,\ this);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnSpeak.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ speakText());} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStop.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (textToSpeech\ !=\ null\ \&\&\ textToSpeech.isSpeaking())\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small textToSpeech.stop();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Called\ once\ the\ TTS\ engine\ is\ initialized} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onInit(int\ status)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (status\ ==\ TextToSpeech.SUCCESS)\ \{} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small int\ result\ =\ textToSpeech.setLanguage(Locale.US);} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (result\ ==\ TextToSpeech.LANG\_MISSING\_DATA\ ||\ result\ ==} \\
{\ttfamily\small TextToSpeech.LANG\_NOT\_SUPPORTED)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Language\ not\ supported",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small isTtsReady\ =\ true;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "TTS\ initialization\ failed",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ speakText()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ text\ =\ etText.getText().toString().trim();} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (TextUtils.isEmpty(text))\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Please\ enter\ some\ text",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (!isTtsReady)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "TTS\ engine\ not\ ready\ yet",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small textToSpeech.speak(text,\ TextToSpeech.QUEUE\_FLUSH,\ null,\ null);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onDestroy()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Release\ TTS\ resources} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (textToSpeech\ !=\ null)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small textToSpeech.stop();} \\
{\ttfamily\small textToSpeech.shutdown();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onDestroy();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

\noindent\begingroup

\rule{0pt}{13pt}Program Title : Create an app to perform asynchronous tasks. \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.asynctaskdemo;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Handler;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Looper;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Button;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.ProgressBar;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.TextView;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Toast;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ java.util.concurrent.Executors;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small ProgressBar\ progressBar;} \\
{\ttfamily\small TextView\ tvProgress,\ tvStatus;} \\
{\ttfamily\small Button\ btnStart;} \\
{\ttfamily\small ExecutorService\ executorService;} \\
{\ttfamily\small Handler\ mainHandler;} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ isTaskRunning\ =\ false;} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
{\ttfamily\small progressBar\ =\ findViewById(R.id.progressBar);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvProgress\ =\ findViewById(R.id.tvProgress);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvStatus\ =\ findViewById(R.id.tvStatus);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStart\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnStart);} \\
{\ttfamily\small executorService\ =\ Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Handler\ tied\ to\ main/UI\ thread} \\
{\ttfamily\small mainHandler\ =\ new\ Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStart.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ startAsyncTask());} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ startAsyncTask()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (isTaskRunning)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Task\ already\ running...",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small isTaskRunning\ =\ true;} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStart.setEnabled(false);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvStatus.setText("Task\ running\ in\ background...");} \\
{\ttfamily\small progressBar.setProgress(0);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvProgress.setText("0\%");} \\
{\ttfamily\small executorService.execute(()\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small for\ (int\ i\ =\ 0;\ i\ \textless{}=\ 100;\ i\ +=\ 5)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small final\ int\ progress\ =\ i;} \\
{\ttfamily\small try\ \{} \\
\strut \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small Thread.sleep(200);\ //\ simulate\ work\ (e.g.\ downloading,\ processing)} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ catch\ (InterruptedException\ e)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small e.printStackTrace();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Post\ progress\ updates\ back\ to\ the\ UI\ thread} \\
{\ttfamily\small mainHandler.post(()\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small progressBar.setProgress(progress);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvProgress.setText(progress\ +\ "\%");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Task\ finished\ —\ update\ UI\ on\ main\ thread} \\
{\ttfamily\small mainHandler.post(()\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvStatus.setText("Task\ completed!");} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnStart.setEnabled(true);} \\
{\ttfamily\small isTaskRunning\ =\ false;} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(MainActivity.this,\ "Background\ task\ finished!",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onDestroy()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onDestroy();} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Shut\ down\ executor\ to\ avoid\ leaks} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (executorService\ !=\ null)\ \{} \\
\strut \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small executorService.shutdownNow();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\}} \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{activity\_main.xml:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}?xml\ version="1.0"\ encoding="utf-8"?\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}LinearLayout\ xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:orientation="vertical"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:gravity="center"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:padding="24dp"\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}TextView} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:text="Async\ Task\ Demo"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:textSize="22sp"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:textStyle="bold"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:paddingBottom="30dp"/\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}TextView} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:id="@+id/tvStatus"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:text="Press\ Start\ to\ begin\ task"} \\

\strut \\

{\ttfamily\small Intent\ intent\ =\ new\ Intent(HomeActivity.this,\ LoginActivity.class);} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG\_ACTIVITY\_CLEAR\_TOP\ |} \\
{\ttfamily\small Intent.FLAG\_ACTIVITY\_NEW\_TASK);} \\

{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.regloginapp;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.content.ContentValues;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.content.Context;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.Cursor;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ DBHelper\ extends\ SQLiteOpenHelper\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ static\ final\ String\ DATABASE\_NAME\ =\ "UserDB";} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ static\ final\ int\ DATABASE\_VERSION\ =\ 1;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ TABLE\_NAME\ =\ "users";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_ID\ =\ "id";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_USERNAME\ =\ "username";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_EMAIL\ =\ "email";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ static\ final\ String\ COL\_PASSWORD\ =\ "password";} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ DBHelper(Context\ context)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super(context,\ DATABASE\_NAME,\ null,\ DATABASE\_VERSION);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onCreate(SQLiteDatabase\ db)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ createTable\ =\ "CREATE\ TABLE\ "\ +\ TABLE\_NAME\ +\ "\ ("\ +} \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_ID\ +\ "\ INTEGER\ PRIMARY\ KEY\ AUTOINCREMENT,\ "\ +} \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_USERNAME\ +\ "\ TEXT\ UNIQUE,\ "\ +} \\
\strut \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_EMAIL\ +\ "\ TEXT,\ "\ +} \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_PASSWORD\ +\ "\ TEXT)";} \\
{\ttfamily\small db.execSQL(createTable);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onUpgrade(SQLiteDatabase\ db,\ int\ oldVersion,\ int\ newVersion)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small db.execSQL("DROP\ TABLE\ IF\ EXISTS\ "\ +\ TABLE\_NAME);} \\
{\ttfamily\small onCreate(db);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ long\ registerUser(String\ username,\ String\ email,\ String\ password)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small SQLiteDatabase\ db\ =\ this.getWritableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small ContentValues\ values\ =\ new\ ContentValues();} \\
{\ttfamily\small values.put(COL\_USERNAME,\ username);} \\
{\ttfamily\small values.put(COL\_EMAIL,\ email);} \\
{\ttfamily\small values.put(COL\_PASSWORD,\ password);} \\
{\ttfamily\small long\ result\ =\ db.insert(TABLE\_NAME,\ null,\ values);} \\
{\ttfamily\small db.close();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return\ result;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ boolean\ isUsernameExists(String\ username)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small SQLiteDatabase\ db\ =\ this.getReadableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small Cursor\ cursor\ =\ db.query(TABLE\_NAME,\ new\ String[]\{COL\_ID\},} \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_USERNAME\ +\ "=?",\ new\ String[]\{username\},\ null,\ null,\ null);} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ exists\ =\ cursor.getCount()\ \textgreater{}\ 0;} \\
{\ttfamily\small cursor.close();} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small db.close();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return\ exists;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ boolean\ checkLogin(String\ username,\ String\ password)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small SQLiteDatabase\ db\ =\ this.getReadableDatabase();} \\
{\ttfamily\small Cursor\ cursor\ =\ db.query(TABLE\_NAME,\ new\ String[]\{COL\_ID\},} \\
{\ttfamily\small COL\_USERNAME\ +\ "=?\ AND\ "\ +\ COL\_PASSWORD\ +\ "=?",} \\
{\ttfamily\small new\ String[]\{username,\ password\},\ null,\ null,\ null);} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ isValid\ =\ cursor.getCount()\ \textgreater{}\ 0;} \\
{\ttfamily\small cursor.close();} \\
{\ttfamily\small db.close();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return\ isValid;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.regloginapp;} \\

{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ RegisterActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ \{} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small EditText\ etUsername,\ etEmail,\ etPassword,\ etConfirmPassword;} \\

{\ttfamily\small DBHelper\ dbHelper;} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_register);} \\
{\ttfamily\small etUsername\ =\ findViewById(R.id.etUsername);} \\
{\ttfamily\small etEmail\ =\ findViewById(R.id.etEmail);} \\
{\ttfamily\small etPassword\ =\ findViewById(R.id.etPassword);} \\
{\ttfamily\small etConfirmPassword\ =\ findViewById(R.id.etConfirmPassword);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnRegister\ =\ findViewById(R.id.btnRegister);} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvGoToLogin\ =\ findViewById(R.id.tvGoToLogin);} \\
{\ttfamily\small dbHelper\ =\ new\ DBHelper(this);} \\
{\ttfamily\small btnRegister.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ registerUser());} \\
{\ttfamily\small tvGoToLogin.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small startActivity(new\ Intent(RegisterActivity.this,\ LoginActivity.class));} \\
{\ttfamily\small finish();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ registerUser()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ username\ =\ etUsername.getText().toString().trim();} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ email\ =\ etEmail.getText().toString().trim();} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ password\ =\ etPassword.getText().toString().trim();} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ confirmPassword\ =\ etConfirmPassword.getText().toString().trim();} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (TextUtils.isEmpty(username)\ ||\ TextUtils.isEmpty(email)\ ||} \\
{\ttfamily\small TextUtils.isEmpty(password)\ ||\ TextUtils.isEmpty(confirmPassword))\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Please\ fill\ all\ fields",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (!password.equals(confirmPassword))\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Passwords\ do\ not\ match",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (dbHelper.isUsernameExists(username))\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Username\ already\ taken",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small long\ result\ =\ dbHelper.registerUser(username,\ email,\ password);} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (result\ !=\ -1)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Registration\ successful!\ Please\ login.",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
{\ttfamily\small startActivity(new\ Intent(RegisterActivity.this,\ LoginActivity.class));} \\
{\ttfamily\small finish();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.makeText(this,\ "Registration\ failed",} \\
{\ttfamily\small Toast.LENGTH\_SHORT).show();} \\
\strut \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\

{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ loginUser()\ \{} \\

{\ttfamily\small if\ (TextUtils.isEmpty(username)\ ||\ TextUtils.isEmpty(password))\ \{} \\

{\ttfamily\small intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG\_ACTIVITY\_CLEAR\_TOP\ |} \\

{\ttfamily\small Intent.FLAG\_ACTIVITY\_NEW\_TASK);} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}Program Title : Create an app to turn on and off the flashlight \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.flashlight;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.camera2.CameraAccessException;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Button;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Button\ flashlightButton;} \\
{\ttfamily\small CameraManager\ cameraManager;} \\
{\ttfamily\small String\ cameraId;} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ isFlashlightOn\ =\ false;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
{\ttfamily\small flashlightButton\ =\ findViewById(R.id.flashlightButton);} \\
{\ttfamily\small cameraManager\ =\ (CameraManager)} \\
{\ttfamily\small getSystemService(CAMERA\_SERVICE);} \\
{\ttfamily\small try\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small cameraId\ =\ cameraManager.getCameraIdList()[0];} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ catch\ (CameraAccessException\ e)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small e.printStackTrace();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small flashlightButton.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small try\ \{} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small isFlashlightOn\ =\ !isFlashlightOn;} \\
{\ttfamily\small cameraManager.setTorchMode(cameraId,\ isFlashlightOn);} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (isFlashlightOn)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small flashlightButton.setText("Turn\ OFF\ Flashlight");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}\ else\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small flashlightButton.setText("Turn\ ON\ Flashlight");} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small catch\ (CameraAccessException\ e)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small e.printStackTrace();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}Program Title : Create an app to change the background color when the phone is shaked or tilted. \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.shakecolor;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.graphics.Color;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.Sensor;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.SensorEvent;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.SensorEventListener;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.hardware.SensorManager;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ java.util.Random;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ implements} \\
{\ttfamily\small SensorEventListener\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small SensorManager\ sensorManager;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small Sensor\ accelerometer;} \\
{\ttfamily\small Random\ random;} \\
{\ttfamily\small float\ lastX,\ lastY,\ lastZ;} \\
{\ttfamily\small boolean\ firstReading\ =\ true;} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
{\ttfamily\small sensorManager\ =\ (SensorManager)\ getSystemService(SENSOR\_SERVICE);} \\
{\ttfamily\small accelerometer\ =} \\
{\ttfamily\small sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE\_ACCELEROMETER);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small random\ =\ new\ Random();} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onResume()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onResume();} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small sensorManager.registerListener(} \\
{\ttfamily\small this,} \\
{\ttfamily\small accelerometer,} \\
{\ttfamily\small SensorManager.SENSOR\_DELAY\_NORMAL} \\
{\ttfamily\small );} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onPause()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onPause();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small sensorManager.unregisterListener(this);} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onSensorChanged(SensorEvent\ event)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small float\ x\ =\ event.values[0];} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small float\ y\ =\ event.values[1];} \\
{\ttfamily\small float\ z\ =\ event.values[2];} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (firstReading)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small lastX\ =\ x;} \\
{\ttfamily\small lastY\ =\ y;} \\
{\ttfamily\small lastZ\ =\ z;} \\
{\ttfamily\small firstReading\ =\ false;} \\
{\ttfamily\small return;} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small float\ change\ =\ Math.abs(x\ -\ lastX)} \\
{\ttfamily\small +\ Math.abs(y\ -\ lastY)} \\
{\ttfamily\small +\ Math.abs(z\ -\ lastZ);} \\
{\ttfamily\small if\ (change\ \textgreater{}\ 10)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small changeBackgroundColor();} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small lastX\ =\ x;} \\
{\ttfamily\small lastY\ =\ y;} \\
{\ttfamily\small lastZ\ =\ z;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small private\ void\ changeBackgroundColor()\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small int\ color\ =\ Color.rgb(} \\
{\ttfamily\small random.nextInt(256),} \\
{\ttfamily\small random.nextInt(256),} \\
{\ttfamily\small random.nextInt(256)} \\
{\ttfamily\small );} \\
{\ttfamily\small findViewById(R.id.mainLayout).setBackgroundColor(color);} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ void\ onAccuracyChanged(Sensor\ sensor,\ int\ accuracy)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\hline

{\ttfamily\small android:id="@+id/mainLayout"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:gravity="center"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:orientation="vertical"\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}TextView} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="wrap\_content"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:text="Shake\ or\ Tilt\ Me!"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:textSize="24sp"\ /\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}/LinearLayout\textgreater{}} \\

\rule{0pt}{13pt}Program Title : Create an app to i) Send an SMS. ii) Send an email. iii) To make a call \\
\hline
\rule{0pt}{13pt}\textbf{MainActivity.java:} \\
\hline
{\ttfamily\small package\ com.example.communicationapp;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.content.Intent;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.net.Uri;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.os.Bundle;} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ android.widget.Button;} \\
{\ttfamily\small import\ androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;} \\
{\ttfamily\small public\ class\ MainActivity\ extends\ AppCompatActivity\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Button\ smsButton,\ emailButton,\ callButton;} \\
{\ttfamily\small @Override} \\
{\ttfamily\small protected\ void\ onCreate(Bundle\ savedInstanceState)\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);} \\
{\ttfamily\small setContentView(R.layout.activity\_main);} \\
{\ttfamily\small smsButton\ =\ findViewById(R.id.smsButton);} \\
{\ttfamily\small emailButton\ =\ findViewById(R.id.emailButton);} \\
{\ttfamily\small callButton\ =\ findViewById(R.id.callButton);} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Send\ SMS} \\
{\ttfamily\small smsButton.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Intent\ intent\ =\ new\ Intent(Intent.ACTION\_SENDTO);} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.setData(Uri.parse("smsto:9876543210"));} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.putExtra("sms\_body",\ "Hello!\ This\ is\ a\ test\ message.");} \\
{\ttfamily\small startActivity(intent);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Send\ Email} \\
{\ttfamily\small emailButton.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Intent\ intent\ =\ new\ Intent(Intent.ACTION\_SENDTO);} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.setData(Uri.parse("mailto:example@gmail.com"));} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA\_SUBJECT,\ "Test\ Email");} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA\_TEXT,\ "Hello!\ This\ is\ a\ test\ email.");} \\
{\ttfamily\small startActivity(intent);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
{\ttfamily\small //\ Make\ Call} \\
{\ttfamily\small callButton.setOnClickListener(v\ -\textgreater{}\ \{} \\
{\ttfamily\small Intent\ intent\ =\ new\ Intent(Intent.ACTION\_DIAL);} \\
{\ttfamily\small intent.setData(Uri.parse("tel:9876543210"));} \\
{\ttfamily\small startActivity(intent);} \\
{\ttfamily\small \});} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\strut \\
{\ttfamily\small \}} \\
\hline

\hline

{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_height="match\_parent"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:gravity="center"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:orientation="vertical"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:padding="20dp"\textgreater{}} \\
{\ttfamily\small \textless{}Button} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:id="@+id/smsButton"} \\
{\ttfamily\small android:layout\_width="match\_parent"} \\
